  %i5329 = tail call reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <4 x half> @f3 [air Math(Math { f: Fma, precision: Precise, ty: NumTy { elem: F16, lanes: 4 } })](%i5328, <4 x half> c321, %i5323)
  %i5330 = insertelement %i5291, %i5298, i64 1
  %i5331 = fmul reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <2 x float> %i5330, %i20
  %i5332 = fadd reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <2 x float> %i5331, %a1
  %i5333 = tail call %t17 @f7 [air Texture(Texture { op: Sample { compare: false, lod: Bias, offset: true }, image: ImageShape { dim: D2, array: false, ms: false, depth: false }, texel: Some(Texel { ty: NumTy { elem: F16, lanes: 4 }, sign: None }), coord16: false, form: Current, status: true, access: Some(Sample), states_access: true })](%a2, %a3, %i5332, i1 1, <2 x i32> zeroinitializer, i1 0, float 0x0, float 0x0, i32 0) convergent arg0( Ref nocapture ) arg1( Ref nocapture )
  %i5334 = extractvalue <4 x half> %i5333, 0
  %i5335 = tail call reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <4 x half> @f3 [air Math(Math { f: Fma, precision: Precise, ty: NumTy { elem: F16, lanes: 4 } })](%i5334, <4 x half> c526, %i5329)
  %i5336 = shufflevector <2 x float> %i5299, <2 x float> poison, <0, 0>
  %i5337 = fmul reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <2 x float> %i5336, %i20
  %i5338 = fadd reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <2 x float> %i5337, %a1
  %i5339 = tail call %t17 @f7 [air Texture(Texture { op: Sample { compare: false, lod: Bias, offset: true }, image: ImageShape { dim: D2, array: false, ms: false, depth: false }, texel: Some(Texel { ty: NumTy { elem: F16, lanes: 4 }, sign: None }), coord16: false, form: Current, status: true, access: Some(Sample), states_access: true })](%a2, %a3, %i5338, i1 1, <2 x i32> zeroinitializer, i1 0, float 0x0, float 0x0, i32 0) convergent arg0( Ref nocapture ) arg1( Ref nocapture )
  %i5340 = extractvalue <4 x half> %i5339, 0
  %i5341 = tail call reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <4 x half> @f3 [air Math(Math { f: Fma, precision: Precise, ty: NumTy { elem: F16, lanes: 4 } })](%i5340, <4 x half> c335, %i5335)
  %i5342 = fmul reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <4 x half> %i5341, %i24
  %i5343 = fadd reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <4 x half> %i5342, %i28
  %i5344 = tail call reassoc nnan ninf nsz arcp contract afn <4 x half> @f4 [air Math(Math { f: Clamp, precision: Precise, ty: NumTy { elem: F16, lanes: 4 } })](%i5343, <4 x half> zeroinitializer, <4 x half> c322)
  %i5345 = add nuw i32 %i5287, i32 1
  %i5346 = icmp eq %i5345, %i5
  br %i5346, bb159, bb160, !llvm.loop LoopHints { unroll: Default, must_progress: true }
bb161:
  unreachable
bb162:
  %i5349 = phi <4 x half> [%i5285, bb159], [%i5144, bb157], [%i5003, bb155], [%i4862, bb153], [%i4721, bb151], [%i4580, bb149], [%i4439, bb147], [%i4298, bb145], [%i4157, bb143], [%i4016, bb141], [%i3875, bb139], [%i3734, bb137], [%i3593, bb135], [%i3452, bb133], [%i3311, bb131], [%i3170, bb129], [%i3029, bb127], [%i2888, bb125], [%i2747, bb123], [%i2606, bb121], [%i2465, bb119], [%i2324, bb117], [%i2183, bb115], [%i2042, bb113], [%i1901, bb111], [%i1760, bb109], [%i1619, bb107], [%i1478, bb105], [%i1337, bb103], [%i1196, bb101], [%i1055, bb99], [%i914, bb97]
  ret %i5349
}
